**One frozen causal rule:** On every completed four-hour LAST bar, use only contiguous history and the previous completed bar's native Wilder ATR(14). Select B as the **earliest maximum high in the most recent 60 completed four-hour bars**, and A as the **latest order-8 pivot low** already confirmed by eight subsequent completed bars, within the most recent 180 bars, before B and at least six bars from B. If absent, emit no setup. Require B no older than **12** completed four-hour bars (H03's already frozen retest horizon). Set 50%, 61.8%, 76.4% to `B - ratio*(B-A)`. ## Diagnostic D38: joint-goal capacity of a stop-avoidance child

**Question and status:** This is a post hoc arithmetic screen on the already exposed H13f aggregate and D37 native exit attribution, not a pre-registered strategy, alternate fill model, or new economic run. Before coding another stop filter, ask whether avoiding the observed losing stops *alone*, while leaving every other recorded trade and account component fixed, could meet both Goal floors. Inputs are H13f `results/2026-10-08-h13f-37-summary.json` SHA-256 `a401a4edd0d0412726b59adf1d96769a0fe6af26c5bdef9711efb78951994f5e` and D37 `results/2026-10-08-h13f-exit-attribution-d37.json` SHA-256 `0ca0903fb582d93466b3acce7953a14e0173b78452ff5b26f1582cc5994b25de`. The account starts at 100,000 USDT and ends at 100,273.62941887; the 2025-10-17 00:00 to 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC reporting window is 0.97357305936 years. There are 64 wins among 177 closed native positions, including 104 losing stop closures totaling -16,906.56872472 USDT.

**Fixed-outcome arithmetic:** Keeping all 64 winners and removing only losers requires removing at least **61 of 113 losses** for a 55% win rate, or **71** for 60%; if only stop closures can be removed, these are respectively **61/104 = 58.65%** and **71/104 = 68.27%** of the observed stops. A strictly greater than 20% annualized return over the exact reporting window requires final equity strictly above **119,423.20655419 USDT**, an increase exceeding **19,149.57713532 USDT** from H13f. Even giving back the *entire* observed stop-loss PnL while leaving all other account components intact yields only **117,180.19814359 USDT**, or **17.69% annualized**. Thus a pure deletion of this fixed set of stop losses cannot by itself meet the return floor, even though such a hypothetical deletion would easily raise the win fraction.

**Decision and limits:** These are necessary-scale calculations for one frozen trade/account path, not a bound on a re-run Strategy: skipped entries alter slot availability, later signals, risk sizing, fees, funding and open equity. A source-grounded H13g or another location child may still be worth testing, but its proposal must say what additional mechanism could create enough *new* winning opportunity, larger payoff, or effective capital use to cross the return gap while retaining a near-60% win rate. If it only promises fewer known stop trades, keep it as a narrow diagnostic child rather than presenting it as a plausible complete Goal candidate. Rank the next experiment by a causal source-case falsifier and its plausible joint-goal effect; do not choose filters or thresholds by the exposed losing positions. Preserve H13f as the exact native paired control. This screen changes no Strategy, historical result, or Goal status.

**Mechanical incompatibility:** With impulse span `D = B - A > 0`, a 50% long entry is `B - 0.5D`, a stop *below* the 76.4% level is `< B - 0.764D`, and target `B` gives reward `0.5D` and risk `> 0.264D`; therefore room is **strictly below `0.5 / 0.264 = 1.89394R`**, even before native price rounding. Its universal `>=2R` room gate would necessarily reject this S24 upper-tier/stop/target combination. This is a scope mismatch between one frozen researcher rule and another author-discussed *conditional style*, not a bug in Nautilus, a proof that the author entered at 50%, or a license to lower the existing H13c/H13f gate after viewing annual PnL.

**Decision:** Preserve H13c/H13f exactly. A distinct prospective 50% entry style could add opportunities rather than merely filter H13f stops, making it relevant to D38's joint-goal gap, but S36 did not verify the prior BTC 50% plan and S24's 50% was already under test when discussed. This rejects the *exact standalone 50%-limit proxy*, not the author's other tier styles or the unresolved causal A selector. Next, before proposing another annual rule, attribute H14a's existing native exits to stop/target/time and test a source-backed context or first-obstacle discriminator against fixed positive and negative source cases; no return-tuned threshold may be chosen from this exposed year.

## Diagnostic D44: can a +1R-only exit rescue H14a's fixed trade-set win-rate gap?

**Registered before price-excursion read:** H14a's 254 wins among 651 native closed positions need at least **105** additional positive outcomes to reach 55%, or **137** to reach 60%, assuming all 651 trades remain and no current win is lost. D43 shows 330 native stop closures with zero wins. **D44 read and decision:** `results/2026-10-08-h14a-exit-opportunity-d44.json` SHA-256 `916b23d4922fe69bbe694dc8d2f9f5b0db2fa8e01b3ac126b4b59f491aad1eb2` matches the frozen H14a native order and position hashes and reconciles **651** closed / **254** positive positions. ## Diagnostic D45: source-stop and tier-room geometry before another native variant

**Registered comparison on already exposed sources, before the new arithmetic read:** H14a uses a standalone 50% limit and `76.4%-0.25*prior_ATR` stop; S27 BTC discusses three separately budgeted tiers with one valid stop below a rising line **or** prior low, while S24 BTC says at least below 76.4%. For each frozen S27 00:00/04:00 and S24 00:00/04:00 cutoff, report A, B, 50/61.8/76.4 tiers, H14a stop, the stop's distance above/below A, target-to-stop R at 50%, and whether an untouched live plan existed. Separately calculate the universal *ceiling* of target-at-B R when a common stop is at or below A for each tier: at stop=A the ceiling is `r/(1-r)` for retracement fraction `r`, and below-A is strictly smaller. Do not digitize an approximate rising line from video pixels, equate A to that line, or call a stop above A invalid under the source's line alternative. If the standalone 1.5R-at-50% floor conflicts with the below-A stop path, treat a common-stop/multiple-tier approach as a **distinct** hypothesis requiring a separately fixed anchor, line/low invalidation, budget, native simultaneous-order protection and capacity gate; do not silently widen H14a's stop or reuse its annual score. Preserve S24's post-touch timing and D41's four SUI anchor matches as unresolved scope, not rescued positives. No fills, PnL or parameter sweep follow from D45.

**D45 read and consequence:** The source-only `audit_h14a_stop_geometry.py` SHA-256 `b9970bd23cd3917bc314e459129c890c1f85b689a749cd1262e7a9cc430c39d5` produced `results/2026-10-08-d45-source-stop-geometry.json` SHA-256 `fbd1fa189f62014569debbb0efb69f4fd3a4ebe778de92b48f3c4317837a2482`. At S24 00:00, the stop **88,704.72** is **1,515.52 above A=87,189.2**, while the 50% tier had already been touched; at 04:00 H14a has no selected recent impulse. Algebraically, if a different common stop is at the selected A, target B provides only **1R** from 50%, **1.6178R** from 61.8% and **3.2373R** from 76.4%; a stop *below* A provides less. Thus H14a's 50%-tier **>=1.5R** condition and a below-A common-stop branch cannot both use B as target. ## Candidate H15a: budgeted three-tier pullback with one below-origin stop

**Registered before implementation, source gate and annual economics:** H14a's standalone 50% plan has 651 closed positions but only 39.02% wins, 1.32 realized payoff and -10.4350% annualized; D44 rules out a +1R-only rescue of its unchanged trade set. S27 explicitly describes optional 50%/61.8%/76.4% entries funded across tiers with a common valid stop below the rising line **or** previous low, while D45 shows that the below-A branch is materially different from H14a's stop. H15a tests the *below selected A* branch as a named researcher proxy. The source does not specify exact allocation fractions or a unique cross-asset A selector; retain those limits instead of claiming author-exactness. This is a potential new opportunity/payoff structure, not a stop tweak or addition of separate H13/H14 PnLs.

**Frozen rule and capacity check:** Reuse H14a's already registered four-hour LAST/ATR A/B selector, pair supersession, 180-bar lookback, latest order-8 confirmed low, earliest prior-60 maximum high, B age <=12, six-bar span, first untouched 50% decision and one live bundle per coin. At a valid completed decision bar, calculate three Instrument-rounded BUY limits at `B-r*(B-A)` for `r=0.5, 0.618, 0.764`; use one fixed common stop `A-0.25*prior_ATR` and first/only target B. Require all three prices and positive stop distance after Instrument rounding; **do not** apply H14a's incompatible 1.5R-to-B per-tier floor. At bundle creation freeze native-equity-based quantity for each tier as Instrument-rounded `min((equity*0.0025/3)/(entry-stop), (equity*0.05/3)/entry)`. Thus the three possible fills share a total **25-bp stop-risk ceiling** and **5% aggregate coin notional cap** at the same account snapshot, with 1x shared margin; unused tier budget is not reassigned. Admit a bundle only if all three native quantities pass exchange minima, and do not change quantities when a later tier fills. Each limit expires after 30 completed four-hour bars; after the first fill, close all filled exposure after 30 such bars if neither common stop nor B target has closed it, and cancel every remaining pending tier on bundle closure. A changed selected A/B before any fill cancels all three limits and waits for native cancel acknowledgment before a replacement. If an old entry fills before those acknowledgments, keep its native protection and cancel the unfinished replacement; already requested sibling cancellations may finish, so do not falsely report a full three-tier fill. Otherwise, after any fill retain the frozen old bundle and any still-active tiers until its closure. The risk ceiling is over all three filled tiers, not 25 bp *per* tier; the max theoretical full-bundle return at B is bounded by the three D45 R ceilings and shrinks with a below-A ATR buffer, fees and cap. This provides *possible* capacity but no forecast or proof of >20% annualized.

**Source, execution and economic gates:** At frozen S27 BTC 2026-04-09 00:00/04:00 UTC, require the selected A/B in the original source bands, all three tiers below the decision close and untouched since B, one proposed bundle with the common stop below A and B as first obstacle. S24 2026-01-08 00:00/04:00 remains a **post-50%-touch sensitivity**, never a newly armed three-tier video order; C19 ETH 2026-04-13 00:00/04:00 remains a wrong-anchor negative; D41/S36 SUI remains a named four-hour A-selector mismatch at all four cutoffs. Failure to form S27 or a false source claim stops this child before annual economics. A pass admits only this scoped researcher proxy. Use Nautilus native entry/exit orders and Portfolio/account state; no parallel fill engine or ledger. Before a real-data pilot, synthetic native event cases must cover three pending limits, one/two/three staggered fills, common-stop or target closure, same-event bar ambiguity, new-pair cancellation acknowledgment, fill-before-cancel, time exit, no residual reduce-only exits and no excess net short. BTC/ETH native pilot then checks order/fill/protection identity and zero denied/rejected orders; pilot PnL cannot select the full replay. If integrity passes, replay all 37 exact existing LAST/MARK/funding Catalogs in one 100,000-USDT account, same eligible window, fees/funding and quantities, plus an exact-input/risk/window H14a control under the same runner regardless of pilot return. Preserve all attempts, source hashes, native raw-report hashes, win/payoff, annualized net equity, Sharpe(365), daily and available minute drawdown, protected opens and paired weekly uncertainty. The year is multiply exposed development data; a successful point estimate still requires delayed/forward and multiplicity-aware qualification before the Goal can complete.

## Diagnostic D49: whether an unfilled tier bundle survives a revisit of its first target

**Registered before this diagnostic reader or result:** D48 found more H16a stops with fewer trades but could not attribute individual paths. S27 names the prior high B as the first obstacle for the pullback plan; S29 says the high is updated after a new effective high. A causal, researcher-defined bundle-retirement question is whether a native plan can remain open after LAST price has already revisited its B target **while none of its tiers has filled**, then fill on a later pullback. This is not an author-stated cancellation command or a new backtest. Bind the exact existing paired H15a/H16a native `orders.csv`, `fills.csv`, `positions.csv` and summaries plus the frozen 37-coin LAST Catalog/input identity. Reconstruct each submitted bundle from its actual native OTO parent and child order IDs, same Instrument, submission time, common stop and B target; reject ambiguous grouping or any parent/child/fill mismatch. For each bundle with a later first native entry fill, inspect only complete five-minute LAST bars **strictly after submission and strictly before the first-fill bar**. Count a pre-fill B revisit only if a bar high reaches the native rounded B target and its low remains above the bundle's highest entry price; this excludes same-bar sequencing and any bar that could itself have filled a tier. Do not use MARK for entry/target touch. Report by H15a and H16a: submitted bundles, first fills, pre-fill B revisits, later filled entries, closed positions and their native closing reason/PnL, and missing/ambiguous paths. Reconcile native fill/position totals to the frozen reports; no per-tier synthetic PnL or cross-strategy order-level counterfactual.

**Decision rule and limits:** If this path is absent or rare, do not prioritize a B-revisit cancellation child as the next Goal-return mechanism. On H15a's fixed 676 closed positions, deleting losses while preserving 382 wins would require at least 40 losses for 60% wins; separately, moving its exact reported final equity above the >20% annualized threshold requires more than **15,262.61 USDT**. Compare the count and absolute loss of *actual* post-revisit losing positions with these two necessary scales. Even exceeding both is only a descriptive capacity screen: cancellation changes later slots, sizing, fees, funding and path, so no old-trade deletion is a tradable return or win-rate forecast. If material, next freeze a source/action rule and Nautilus-native cancellation lifecycle including cancel-before-fill race and a full same-account paired replay; if not, retain this exact failure attribution and seek a source-supported new opportunity/first-obstacle mechanism rather than changing tier weights on the exposed year. Preserve all reads as development exposure; nothing here qualifies the Goal.

**D49 native path result, coverage repair and branch choice:** Preregistration commit `4def2aaeb` preceded reader freeze `945773682`. The first result `results/2026-10-08-d49-b-revisit-before-fill-attempt1.json` SHA-256 `818fdab0dadd7e992b946d5ec6c5f5e500139da0738fc51bebc25df7bcb0a5e8` grouped orders by stripping `USDT-` from InstrumentId, silently missing the `1000PEPE` and `1000SHIB` aliases; its 29 H15a path count is a **failed coverage claim**. Its title is only a selection clue; year, author, market, advice and rule are unverified until original source read. Acquire canonical `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FLg_WR2ZAFE` once via the existing fast `video-note-mcp` CLI, retaining receipt, original media, full transcript and original frames or an exact stage failure. Check date and duplicate media identity against retained S01-S41 sources before using content. The Goal's viewed 2025-10 to 2026-10 data remain exposed; a video publication date inside or outside that period does not create a financial holdout.

**Fixed source questions and gate:** Read the whole decision segment and its original chart frames, not the title alone. Determine whether the speaker refers to an already held **futures** position, spot holding, or a new entry; what observable state the title's unnamed object denotes; whether it was known before the suggested action; whether the relevant candle must be complete; and whether he explicitly maps persistence/loss of that state to **hold, reduce or close** with a stop or obstacle. Use existing S35's weak-rebound-but-line-intact case as a negative for automatically exiting a slow winner, and S40's prior-high futures profit-taking case as a negative for carrying H15a unconditionally beyond B. A source-positive conditional-exit lead needs a named pre-visible condition, an actionable response and a distinguishable negative case. If speech only says to watch a level or retrospectively explains the drop, record source-inconclusive and do not encode a native Strategy from it. Any causal numerical proxy still needs its own fixed source-case and native event-capacity gate before annual economics. This source read has no orders, account score or permission to fit thresholds on the viewed year.

**Original read and decision:** Registration was committed at `5dca69e62` before acquisition. The first unchanged CLI attempt returned `SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE/youtube_acquisition_failed`; direct metadata/media-worker diagnostics succeeded and the second complete pipeline attempt produced original media, a full 427-second fast-ASR transcript and original frames. The retained [S42 evidence record](results/2026-10-08-s42-held-position-source.json) binds both receipts, all manifest/note SHA-256 values and the media SHA-256; its media hash had no match in the retained S01-S41 result artifacts. The source identifies the official Ronnie channel and 2026-07-09 publication. The BTC title is not a BTC held-futures exit rule: at 00:03-00:57 (E001-E020, frame F41), he calls the current range middle random and advises waiting for deeper levels before a **new** long. At 01:57-02:52 (E045-E071, frames F13/F45), he describes SOL's four-hour prior-resistance-as-support retest near the displayed 38.2% retracement and tells existing longs to **continue holding** (E063-E064); the stop at least below 50% is explicitly for someone **entering now** (E065-E071), not an instruction to close the held trade if 38.2% fails. At 04:51-05:49 (E132-E159, frame F16), he tells existing XAUUSD shorts to hold while a descending line still caps price, without specifying an early close on a later line break; XAUUSD is outside the 37-coin futures universe. Neither source case specifies a unique causal anchor, completed-candle clock and reduce/close action on invalidation. Thus S42 is a genuine **persistence/slow-winner counterexample** but fails the preregistered conditional-exit source gate. Do not encode a Ronnie-attributed exit, infer a BTC rule from the title, transplant the SOL new-entry stop onto an existing H15a position, or rerun the exposed year for this source-only read. S35 and S40 remain distinct hold and target boundaries. The next source probe must search specifically for a pre-visible structure-loss response on an already held crypto futures position; any researcher proxy would still need its own native-event capacity screen before paired economics.
